{"forms":[{"form":"cribbings","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The members used to build a (structural) crib, usually of timbers or logs, but also of concrete, steel or even plastic; cribwork."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"After the Loma Prieta earthquake, they had to put cribbing under portions of San Francisco's Embarcadero Freeway, for fear it would collapse.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As a whole, the heavy structure built to support an existing structure from underneath, as with a mineshaft or when raising a building off its foundation, as for moving to another location,"],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The cribbing used to support anything from below or on a side, as with a retaining wall, or to prop up a piece of heavy machinery."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A self-injurious tendency of certain horses to swallow air while slobbering and biting onto objects in and about their enclosure; cribbing and windsucking are regarded as equine forms of obsessive-compulsive disorder."],"qualifier":"ethology","tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["equestrianism","hobbies","horses","lifestyle","pets","sports"]},{"glosses":["An act of plagiarism."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈkɹɪbɪŋ/"}],"upstream_line":546029,"upstream_offset":2495609436,"upstream_record_sha256":"87e5b93b4350553368209eed9f48e7379e6b8cd29f01eb2e6ae00dab29c60419","word":"cribbing"}
